> 2026-11-13: **VOID PHASE 1 (server core) BUILT, PR open, NOT released** (branch `claude/void-p1-1f3e7a`, migration 0092, backend only). Owner said "Yes, start Void". Plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-12-void-plan.md` Tasks 1.1-1.6.
> - **Built:** `order_voids` / `order_void_items` / `order_void_followups` (append-only triggers), `orders.void_id` + `replaces_order_id`, ledger kind `void` (also in the 5 OpenAPI kind enums, Backoffice labels, stock card), `void_window_days` setting (default 7, 1-30), `void_own_shift` granted to cashier users and kasir/cashier custom roles (O1), points type `void`. `service/void.go` (`VoidOrderTx`): stock back from the sale's own ledger movements (giveaway included), points restore-then-claw (redeemed points are given back first, so the reversal is exact whenever balance + redeemed >= earned; the rest is floored and recorded as forgiven), code release, customer spend, visit and `last_visit` (moved back to the latest remaining non-voided order, or cleared when none remains, only when the voided sale was the latest visit), stock-count choice per line, idempotent replay, never writes `order_refunds`. Sync item `void`, `POST /orders/{id}/void` (needs `void_any`, `clientVoidUuid` required), `GET /orders/{id}/void-preview`, `Order.void` / `replacesOrderId` on reads (merged with `jsonb ||`: `json_build_object` was at the 100-argument cap), `voidWindowDays` in settings, movement summary counts a void once.
> - **ERP (O4):** void documents and any replacement sale's documents are `held` / `void_erp_pending` with a finite `next_attempt_at`; `claimSQL` and admin Retry skip them. A stockless void has no outbox row (Phase 5 backfills from `order_voids.erp_document_key`).
> - **What the owner can do after release:** a void is possible through the API only. There is NO POS or Backoffice button until Phases 3 and 4, and the Backoffice permission picker does not list `void_any` until Task 4.4 (grant it in the database until then). Voids of sales in closed shifts are refused until Phase 2 (`ErrVoidAfterCloseNotYet`). ERP documents for voids stay held until Phase 5 (blocked on the ERP developer's Q1-Q4).
> - **CI:** new Postgres gate `TestVoidPostgres` (prefix VD, port 26482); the range is now 26439-26482 in `postgres_gate.py`, `postgres.yml`, `runner-provision.sh`, `scripts/ci/README.md`. A self-hosted runner needs the sysctl re-applied (owner/infra step).
> - **Rollback warning:** once a void exists, do NOT roll the backend back past this revision without handling the data first. The previous revision's relay would claim the held `void` outbox rows (due `next_attempt_at`), fail them as an unknown kind (dead), and its ledger summary errors on kind `void`. Roll forward instead, or park the `held` rows first.
> - **Known limits (accepted, in the PR):** the migration's `VALIDATE` gives no lock relief (one transaction per file); the Backoffice still shows Retry on held void rows (server refuses); a count still open (in progress / awaiting approval) now also forces the stock choice.
> - **NEXT:** owner review and release (backend first), then Phase 2 (replacement and closed-shift corrections, migration 0093).

> 2026-11-13: **FIX (#148) LIVE as POS OTA 2.16.0: combinable standalone discount codes now stack with every combinable promotion, Diskon Bundle included** (owner: "Yes, all promotions", "Yes, release both"; wave merge `e31621a2`). OTA 2.16.0: id `a3b17f04`, 309764 B, journal guard ok (base `cdcfa44`); sha256 `1aa0f165...` matches the download; `/api/v1/ota/check` code 14 -> 2.16.0, code 13 -> "no update". Existing standalone codes marked combinable now stack on tablets running 2.16.0. (owner decision 2026-11-12). Root cause: POS `decideApplyCode` returned an unlinked code without `combinable`, so the reducer treated every standalone code as exclusive and evicted the whole set (and `reconcileAutoPromos` held auto promos back).
> - **Fix:** `promoValidate.ts` carries `combinable: dc.combinable ?? false` on unlinked codes. Go already gates on the code row's flag, so no Go logic change; shared golden `combinability_golden.json` (Go `TestCombinabilityGolden` + POS `combinabilityParity.test.ts`). An exclusive code still blocks everything.
> - **Widening approved:** owner, 2026-11-13, "Yes, all promotions" (relayed by the coordinating session). A combinable standalone code stacks with EVERY combinable promotion type (bundle, BXGY, tiered, auto), not just Diskon Bundle; the server already allowed all of these. Existing standalone codes marked combinable START STACKING once the OTA lands.
> - **Ships as:** POS OTA only (no backend deploy). Opus review: no CRITICAL/HIGH.

> 2026-11-10: **LOAD TEST STEP 1 (LOCAL) DONE**. PR #139 (harness `loadtest/` + `docs/qa/2026-11-10-load-test-local.md`), not merged.
> - **Setup:** 30 outlets, 500k customers, 1M orders over a year, on laptop Docker; one backend with `DB_MAX_CONNS` 10; rate limits raised for the test only.
> - **No lost or duplicate sale: PASS.** The 60-tablet burst of 3,000 sales landed exactly once, the replay added 0, and stock was exact.
> - **Upload p95 < 1 s: FAIL under the full burst.**
>   - 18 s with 60 tablets + 5 Backoffice users; 8.5 s without the Backoffice users. All 3,000 were accepted in about 46 s, at about 88-179 sales/s.
>   - 0.9 s with 1 tablet, 1.3 s with 10.
> - **Lists and reports p95 < 2 s: lists PASS (0.4-1.4 s); reports FAIL** (summary 2.1 s, dashboard 5.8 s).
> - **Month export < 30 s: FAIL** (1,740 s).
> - **Root cause, confirmed by Opus in code:** `count(*) OVER()` in `read/orders.go:66` (also customers, catalog, error logs, goods receipts and others) builds every row of the window on every page.
>   - Measured fix: page 1 3,261 ms -> 10 ms plus a separate count; page 200 2,670 ms -> 31 ms with id-first pagination.
> - **Reports:** need a daily rollup (not prototyped).
> - **Sale path:** about 31 statements per sale; re-measure on Neon before optimising.
> - **Incident:** the Docker VM (3.8 GB) dropped both containers mid-run; they were restarted and the data is intact. A local artifact.
> - **`orders.order_number` not unique:** by design since migration 0005, not a finding.

> 2026-11-10: **INSTANT PERMISSION REVOCATION (#138) LIVE: backend `00179-duv` (mig 0087), Backoffice `01b98f0e`, POS OTA 2.13.9** (owner: "Use your recommendations", "Yes, release it").
> - **Release:**
>   - **Merge and gate:** merge `39ef5ec5`; gate `gate/revoke` 5/5 green.
>   - **Backend:** `deploy-backend.sh revoke1110` -> `mandalika-pos-00179-duv`, promoted to 100% in one step. ROLLBACK `00177-hab` (safe: the old revision ignores the column and the claim).
>     - Startup logged "migrations applied" with no errors (0087 is not named in the log; Claude does not sign in to Neon to confirm).
>     - Tag and live `/readyz` 200. `/auth/me` without a token -> 401. 0 ERROR logs; no unexpected 401s after promote.
>   - **Backoffice:** published by `01b98f0e` `[skip actions]`. Cloudflare green; the live crawl finds "sesi diperbarui, coba lagi".
>   - **POS OTA 2.13.9:** id `5c87a7bb`, 303697 B, sha256 `ceca811b...` matches the download. `/ota/check`: code 14 -> 2.13.9; code 13 -> up-to-date.
>     - The journal guard first refused, using its default base `c7e71e53` (the last versionCode bump). It flagged `ClassicPrinterPlugin.java`, which the real APK 2.3 build `cdcfa44e` already contains, so it was re-run with `APK_BUILD_SHA=cdcfa44e` -> "journal guard ok" (no ACK needed).
>     - On the test tablet: 2.13.9 `pending`, then the in-app "Mulai ulang sekarang", then `success`. Online and synced on the new backend with its pre-deploy token.
> - **Live revoke round trip:** the owner tested it on 2026-11-10 (removed a permission from a signed-in manager as Admin) and reported it passed. The AU-gate subtests 11-21 cover it on Postgres too. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-10-instant-permission-revoke.md`.
> - **What:** `users.perm_version` (mig 0087) and access-token claim `pv`; `RequireAuth` refuses `pv < current` or a deleted user with a refreshable 401 `token revoked` (5 s memo, then Redis `authpv:<uid>`, then Postgres). Every rights write bumps it (user update when role/outlet/permissions really change, password reset, delete = tombstone, fsmigrate), guarded by a source-scan test.
> - **Open shift:** deleting a user, or moving their outlet, is refused with 409 while they have an open shift.
> - **Clients:** a 401 right after a successful refresh refreshes once more and never signs out (shared `packages/api` client, POS needs an OTA); the Backoffice adopts the refreshed rights.
> - **Release order:** backend first (old clients already refresh on a 401), then Backoffice, then the POS OTA (it can wait: without it a cashier can be signed out only by two admin edits inside one refresh-retry window).

> 2026-11-10: **BLUETOOTH CLASSIC PRINTING (#136) LIVE: APK 2.3 (versionCode 14), VERIFIED ON THE TABLET** (owner: "Yes, plan it", "Yes, start it", "Yes, release it").
> - **Why:** the owner's printer "RPPO2N" (Rongta RPP02N) is bonded DUAL with only the serial-port profile (SPP). It never answers BLE GATT service discovery (logcat `GATTC_Discover`, then `gatt_rsp_timeout` at 30 s), and the POS printed over BLE only, so it showed "Connection timeout.".
> - **Plan:** `docs/plans/2026-11-10-bluetooth-classic-printing.md`.
> - **Change:**
>   - `ClassicPrinter` Java plugin: RFCOMM on its own executor, with deadlines on connect and write.
>   - `AutoBluetoothTransport` routes each bonded printer to SPP or BLE at connect time, so there's no re-pairing. BT_OFF and PERMISSION_DENIED surface as Indonesian copy.
>   - Print jobs are serialized (`runExclusive`).
>   - Print errors are Indonesian and start with "Gagal".
>   - `printer.tsx` `supported` = kind != none.
>   - APK 2.3 / versionCode 14.
> - **Review:** Opus approve-with-fixes; all 6 fixes were applied in `82401deb` and checked by Opus.
>   - Capacitor 8.5.3 API use was verified against the real sources.
>   - Gradle cannot run locally (sandbox loopback), so the APK build is the first real Java compile.
> - **Merge:** `e401ec70` (no-ff on `fe40c69c`). Gate `gate/bt-classic` 5/5 green; pushed behind `[CF-Pages-Skip]` `cdcfa44e`. No backend or Backoffice change.
> - **APK:** `android-release.yml` run `42754707726` (test). The build compiled the new Java against the real Capacitor 8.5.3.
>   - Artifact `mandalika-pos-2.3-build14-test-cdcfa44`; the manifest (commit `cdcfa44e`, 2.3 / 14) sha256 `11fb7cbf...` matches the download.
>   - Installed with `adb install -r` on SM-X115 (0 unsent, empty cart): 2.2/13 → 2.3/14. Login and shift were kept; the updater shows builtin 2.3.
>   - Before the install the tablet showed OTA 2.13.8 `success`, so #135 is confirmed on a device.
> - **Verified on the device with the RPP02N** (the owner confirmed the paper):
>   - **Tes Cetak:** logcat shows an RFCOMM socket on UUID 0x1101 and no BLE GATT attempt. The Bluetooth Classic link plus service lookup take about 6 s cold; the next print is about 0.2 s.
>   - **Reprint** of AP0005 from History.
>   - **Test sale BSD-AP0009:** 8 lines of test products, Rp 710.000 cash. It auto-printed; "Cetak Ulang Struk" pressed twice ran strictly one after the other.
>   - 5 receipts printed in full.
>   - **Printer off:** after 10.2 s, "Gagal mencetak: printer tidak menjawab…" in red. Meanwhile other native plugin calls answered in 7–13 ms, so the plugin thread is not blocked.
>   - **Bluetooth off:** "Gagal mencetak: Bluetooth tablet mati…" in red, 0.2 s after the tap.
>   - **Recovery:** Bluetooth and the printer back on, and Tes Cetak printed in 2.1 s.
>   - **Cleanup:** AP0009 fully refunded ("QA-APK23-print test sale cleanup"). History shows Refund, QA stock is back to 16, and the tablet is synced. This was checked on the tablet, not through the live API.
> - **NOT done:** other tablets are still on APK 2.1/2.2 and need 2.3 for printing and future OTAs.
> - **After this:** OTAs target versionCode 14, so tablets on APK 2.2 get no further OTAs until they install 2.3.
> - **Not covered:** a BLE printer (none available) and iOS.

> - **F22** (partial): owner-close and owner-reload hand over, but navigating the owner tab to another https site leaves the waiter blocked until that tab closes. `tabGuard.ts` holds the lock until document destruction, and nothing releases it on departure. > - **F35**: live `settings.receipt` stores only `showCashier:false` and `showTax:false`. Backoffice draws the six missing flags OFF (`!!flag`, shallow `normalize`). The printer treats five of them as ON (`!== false`) and three as OFF (truthy). The preview ignores the customer and change flags and never shows the phone. Slice `r11-receipt`: one shared per-key resolver whose defaults equal the printer's current output, so paper does not change; change shown outside the receipt mock when `showChange` is off.
> - **F36**: the preview uses `new Date()` on every render. Same slice: one time source, the order's recorded client time, for both preview and print.

> 2026-11-08: **SYNC FLAGS BUILT, PR open, not merged/deployed/OTA'd** (follow-ups of the QA waves; session fu-sync-flags). (1) Negative opening cash on the sync batch: a queued `shift_open` with startingCash < 0 is still accepted and stored AS SENT (never dropped), but is now flagged: migration 0086 adds `shifts.starting_cash_warning` (flag-only boolean, the `orders.*_warning` pattern; the migration also flags shifts that already landed negative, e.g. Neon c5476114 at -1), set at the sync call site; `Shift.startingCashWarning` (additive OpenAPI, regenerated Go + TS) feeds a red `Kas awal negatif` badge beside the cashier in Laporan > Shift (hover shows the amount). The direct `POST /shifts` still returns 400. `sync_review_items` was NOT used: it only holds items the tablet parked after a refusal. (2) Split-refund payout audit: the POS refund payload gets an additive `payoutPicked` (split orders only, always next to `payoutMethod`): true = the cashier tapped a chip, false = the preselected default was kept. Server maps it to `order_refunds.payout_source`: 'default' only when payoutPicked is false AND the sent method equals the server's own D-56 default for the order (owner call after the security review, so a client claim can never relabel a different method); every other case, incl. picked true, a mismatch, a missing or non-boolean field, stays 'chosen'; the method and every amount are unchanged, so old bundles and already-queued payloads behave exactly as before. A `null` payoutPicked reads as absent (a unit test caught `null` decoding as false). Reviewer follow-ups applied: the read model also reports the flag for any negative starting cash (covers a negative shift the old revision stores during the deploy window); the OpenAPI payoutSource/payoutPicked text is updated. Plan agreed by the Opus planner (with changes: raw-JSON decode so a bad `payoutPicked` can never reject a refund; flag set at the call site; backfill in the migration; `SyncRefundPayload.payoutPicked` documented in OpenAPI). **Journal guard: no guarded file touched** (changed: refundView.ts, RefundDialog.tsx, CashierScreen.tsx; none on `scripts/lib/ota-journal-guard.sh`), so the OTA needs no journal ack and an OTA rollback only drops the new key. **Release order: backend first (migration 0086 + flag + payoutPicked; the old backend ignores unknown payload keys, so POS-before-backend would be safe too), then POS OTA, backoffice any time after the backend.** Migration renumbered 0085 -> 0086 because the points-redemption branch owns 0085. Not run locally: Postgres integration tests (no local DB/Docker; they compile and run in CI) and `codegen_gate.py` (needs Go 1.26.10); CI covers both.

Read-only: no sale, refund or shift close; shift still open afterwards. How-to for future tablet checks: memory `tablet-checks-via-adb` (adb at `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Android\Sdk\platform-tools`, CDP forward, never type credentials).

> 2026-11-05 (**customer search finds a number in any spelling**, branch `claude/customer-phone-search`, PR against prod-hardening, NOT merged; **backend deploy only, migration 0082, no POS/OTA/APK**). Follow-up to #67: the backoffice list `GET /customers?q=` and the reader typeahead `GET /sync/customers/lookup?q=` matched the raw `phone` text only, so a customer stored `0812...` was not found by `62812...` or `+62 812...`. New `phone.SearchKeys(q)`: phone-shaped input (digits after stripping spaces/-/./()/leading + or 00) also matches `phone_norm`: a number written with a prefix is a prefix search (`0812-34`, `62812 34`, `+62 812 34` -> `'081234%'`; `+65 8123` -> `'658123%'`, so a foreign number is never read as Indonesian), bare digits (4+) are a substring (`'%8123%'`, a bare 62 is a country code only when 8 follows); too few digits adds nothing. Name/email/raw-phone ILIKE kept, so nothing that matched stops matching; the typeahead ranks an exact `phone_norm` hit first. Migration 0082 = partial GIN trigram index on `phone_norm` (plain CREATE INDEX like 0026/0080: the runner is transactional). Searched elsewhere: the POS finder + offline known-customers are full-phone-only and already compare `phone_norm`/`normalizePhone`, the cashier lookup stays phone-only exact, so no TS change. Tests: `TestSearchKeys` (Go table) + new subtests in the CP Postgres gate (admin and manager, list and typeahead, full/partial/`+62`/foreign `+65`, merged twin hidden, cashier still 403/empty); gate green locally native PG 18.3. EXPLAIN at 500k rows in the PR body.

> 2026-10-29 (C5 "Versi baru siap" OTA prompt, D-19, PR open, NOT merged/OTA'd). **WEB-LAYER ONLY: no APK, no `capacitor.config.ts` change, no versionCode bump, no backend, no migration.** The plan's manual-mode assumption was wrong: with `autoUpdate: true` Capgo v8.50 already emits `updateAvailable{bundle}` and queues the bundle (`setNextBundle`); `getNextBundle()` reads the queue; `reload()` applies it (documented for a "Restart now" button). Checked in the v8.50.2 types, the Android source and context7.
> Code: `otaUpdate.ts` (Capgo glue, native-only, no-op on web, never throws), `lib/otaUpdateController.ts` (state + restart, re-reads the gate at tap time), `lib/otaApplyGate.ts` (pure gate), `hooks/useOtaUpdate.ts`; SyncStatus sheet section + a quiet info Banner in CashierScreen. "Mulai ulang sekarang" is enabled only with: empty cart, no checkout open/processing/success, no refund flow, no sale/refund/customer/receive/shift write in flight (live single-flight refs read at tap). Otherwise disabled with the reason; a blocked tap toasts the reason. `notifyAppReady` still has its one caller (native.ts); no A35 journal-guarded file touched, so NO `OTA_JOURNAL_CHANGE_ACK` needed. `/ota/check` unchanged.
> Opus review (high): ship-with-fixes; fixed the shift-write gap, result naming and blocked-tap feedback. **KNOWN GAP, own slice (pre-existing, NOT fixed here):** Capgo's background apply (`appMovedToBackground` -> `installNext`) still reloads with no sale gate, so switching to an e-wallet/EDC app mid-checkout can reload the WebView once an update is queued (queued sales are safe; the cart and open checkout are lost). The fix is the `kill` delay so only the gated button applies. Then FIXED in this PR (owner-approved): `armOtaKillDelay()` at the top of `boot()` + on every `updateAvailable`. Capgo applies `next` only on background, never on a cold start, so blocking the background apply alone would strand tablets on the old bundle. OWNER DECISION (a), built: `startupOta()` (awaited first in `boot()`, before storage hydrate and any UI/cart) arms the delay and applies a queued bundle ONCE on a cold start via `reload()`; skipped when nothing usable is queued (none/builtin/errored), the queued id is the running one, the current bundle is not yet `success` (unconfirmed), or the per-id localStorage marker `mandalika.pos.otaColdApplyTried` (written BEFORE the reload) shows it was tried (no boot loop after a failed native reload). All bridge waits are bounded; after requesting a reload boot waits up to 8 s (2 s after a rejection) so storage hydrate never starts in a dying page. Note: Android often kills the process while the cashier is in an EDC/e-wallet app, so coming back is a cold start and the update applies then (the in-memory cart is lost; parked orders persist). A queued builtin is never applied (the backend never sends one). The 1-2 s pre-arm window is moot for the background path now: the apply happens at cold start, before any cart exists. Also open: the OTA gate and `useAutoPullGate` flags can drift (no shared predicate). On-device check owed: publish an OTA, the prompt appears after the download, the button is disabled with a cart, a restart applies and the app comes back without a rollback.

- **Deploy pattern:** CI green on the pushed commit → `scripts/deploy-backend.sh <tag>` (refuses unless clean + on
  origin/prod-hardening + all 4 gates green; deploys a clean export of that SHA, labels the revision `commit-sha`; traffic-free;
  prints rollback) → smoke the tag URL → `scripts/promote-backend.sh <tag>` (re-checks the label's commit is CI-green, then `/readyz`). APK builds use **origin**: push first, then
  `gh workflow run android-release.yml --ref prod-hardening -f api_url=https://mandalika-pos-538849926142.asia-southeast2.run.app`.
  The release workflow re-runs the PG gate; Docker Hub rate limits ("toomanyrequests") → `gh run rerun <id> --failed`.
- **CI:** backend (`-race`, **gofmt** — committed files must be gofmt-clean; CRLF working copies look dirty locally,
  check with `git show HEAD:<f> | gofmt -l`), Mandatory PostgreSQL (11 gates incl. RF refund parity), JS/TS
  (`corepack pnpm quality:ci`), Chromium browser gate (`corepack pnpm browser:ci`). dependency-security fails on
  24 pre-existing blockers (not a deploy gate). Local PG gate:
  `python scripts/ci/postgres_gate.py --runtime native --pg-bin "C:/Program Files/PostgreSQL/18/bin" --no-race --output <f>.json`.
- **Git hook:** blocks any command line containing `-n`/`--no-…` tokens (also inside commit text, e.g. "pre-native").
  Write commit messages to a file and use `git commit -F <file>`; run greps separately.
- **Hosted checks:** smoke scripts use Node fetch with secrets from Secret Manager (never printed). Neon read-only:
  `psql "$(gcloud secrets versions access latest --secret=DATABASE_URL --project mandalika-pos)"` with `BEGIN READ ONLY`.

## Emulator

AVD `mandalika-tab` (Android 15, `userdebug` → `adb root` works). Start cold:
`"%LOCALAPPDATA%\Android\Sdk\emulator\emulator.exe" -avd mandalika-tab -no-snapshot-load`. **Close it before the PC
sleeps** (`adb emu kill`, then stop leftover `emulator.exe`). APK 1.8 installed, admin logged in, terminal outlet = Mandalika BSD, shift open, queue empty.
- DNS often breaks after a cold boot: re-apply Private DNS
  (`settings put global private_dns_mode off`, then `hostname`, `private_dns_specifier dns.google`).
- Read the WebView, not screenshots: `adb forward tcp:9222 localabstract:webview_devtools_remote_<pid>` + a small CDP
  `Runtime.evaluate` helper. Queue: `await Capacitor.Plugins.OutboxJournal.readAll()`; `open()` must report
  `synchronous: 2`, `journalMode: "truncate"`. `mandalika.pos.outbox` in Preferences is only the sentinel now.
- Claude may not type passwords/PINs — the owner logs in (admin `admin@mandalika.test`).
- Test sales: Holy Sweet 10ml (Rp 225.000) at BSD. Receipt numbers BSD-G0010..13 now exist twice on the server
  (June test orders + 2026-10-11 tests) — the known receipt-number reuse, not duplicates.

## Decisions (owner)

- 2026-11-10 (instant permission revocation, "Use your recommendations"): **supersedes A33 D1 ("token_version is not added") and the "role change within 15 min" note.** (1) Deleting a user with an open shift is refused (409); a "nonaktifkan" state is a later slice. (2) A password reset signs the user out at once (it bumps `perm_version`). (3) Moving a user to another outlet while they have an open shift is refused (409). (4) Refund authority is unchanged: the A06 send-time rule stays (judging at capture time would trust a tablet clock and weaken a refund guard). Also: 5 s memo + 2 min Redis TTL accepted; logout does not bump; fsmigrate bumps.
- 2026-10-10: shared terminal account; no POS outlet switcher/logout; never wipe an unsynced queue; old-format events
  accepted one release / 14 days then review; cash below total = reject online / accept-and-flag offline; denied offline
  refund = visible review item, never deleted; competing transfer receipt = first wins; tablets at 0 pending before
  sync-changing deploys and APK upgrades.
- 2026-10-11 (slices 6/8): tender ±Rp1; per-method `isCash`; TUNAI blank = exact; offline "Buka Shift" blocked; admin
  picks terminal outlet once; late sales keep their closed shift; reset password rules.
- 2026-10-11 (slice 7): already-refunded/cancelled → refuse → review; refund cash recorded on the refunding shift
  (mig 0047, refunded_by); refund paid back in the original method; backoffice refunds → no drawer; POS refund needs an
  open shift; reason ≥1 non-space, ≤500; tablet remembers synced refunds; server holds shift_close AND shift_open
  behind an earlier temporary failure in the same batch.
- 2026-10-11 (slice 9): journal failure before takeover → keep selling on old storage + warning; after takeover → stop;
  in-repo SQLite plugin; transfer PIN stays in the queued item (follow-up: one-time server approval); OTA strictly newer;
  backup excludes journal + Preferences + WebView/Capgo state; debug logging off; OTA "builtin" risk = release rule now.
- 2026-10-12: **head office** resolves review items (see + mark handled, no approve-and-apply); resolved items are removed
  from the tablet; new permission `resolve_sync_review`; refresh-token TTL **30 days** (`JWT_REFRESH_TTL_HOURS=720`, ships
  with the next backend deploy); **APK 1.7 rollout held** for 1.8 (= 1.7 + review reporting); debug flag stays on until go-live.

- 2026-10-14 (audit-sweep answers): **finish every non-policy open finding before the Fable re-audit**; findings that need
  owner policy (A16–A22 loyalty/returns/exchanges/offline inventory, A08 offline transfer approval) wait for the re-audit.
  APK 1.9 is NOT rolled out until production-ready; legacy-envelope cutoff = go-live checklist step.
  - **A36 backups:** prod will leave the Neon free tier. Keep backups **1–2 years**; acceptable data loss **< 3%** (owner's words —
    translate into a concrete RPO/RTO with the owner, e.g. PITR ≤ 15 min + daily off-host logical dumps). Research POS DB-management
    best practice + best value managed Postgres/backup services and recommend before building.
  - **A14 stock count:** add an already-completed guard (no double apply). Stock moving during a count must not block operations:
    apply the **difference** — final = current + (counted − frozen). Owner example: frozen 15, 3 sold during count → 12; counted 14
    → 11, counted 16 → 13. Caveat to design for: counts must reflect stock at freeze/count time (per-item count timestamp).
  - **A33 Redis:** owner unsure → recorded default: tablets sell offline through at least a full trading day with no degradation;
    a Redis outage must never log anyone out or block server startup — Postgres is the auth authority, Redis a cache only.
  - **A39 CSV:** import is a real onboarding feature (initial catalog/customers) → fix properly (standards parser, preview, row
    errors, formula-safe export).
  - **A44 short stock:** never block a sale (negative stock allowed, matches allowNegativeStock default) but **flag and report**
    every oversold line so auditor/cashier human errors surface.
- **2026-10-17 owner policy answers (medium-findings scoping):**
  1. **A05 offline authority:** offline sales allowed until shift close, capped at 12 h after the last successful online contact;
     enforced on next sync (recommendation accepted).
  2. **A27 refunds in reports:** recognised on the REFUND date.
  3. **A28 time:** TWO timezones in use, WIB and WITA (not WIB-only) → each outlet needs a timezone; the business day belongs to
     the SHIFT'S OPENING date; NO return deadline until the owner sets one.
  4. **A30 customer privacy:** cashiers look customers up BY PHONE only (no browsing); managers see their own brand's customers;
     admins see all.
  5. **A41 local receipts:** keep 7 days OR the last 500 receipts on the tablet; offline returns for sales made on that tablet
     before reconnecting = YES.
  6. **A08 transfer-receive PIN:** NOT approvable offline → online step-up; stop shipping PIN hashes in the sync bundle; remove
     the raw PIN from the queued payload; server-side lockout.
  7. **A37 error_logs:** retention 60 days, admin-only read. **IMPLEMENTED on branch a37-error-logs (not pushed/deployed):**
     redaction twins `packages/sync/src/redact.ts` + `backend/internal/redact` share one vector file
     (`packages/sync/src/__fixtures__/redact-vectors.json`). Two tiers: SECRETS (credential keys, Bearer/JWT/URL-secret patterns;
     used for review reports to head office, which keep customer data) and LOGS (secrets + PII keys, email/phone patterns, size
     caps; used for error logs, the sync log and the cashier "Kirim log" diagnostics). The server redacts inside
     `write.InsertErrorLogs` (covers old POS bundles and the panic path) and the Sentry event text; 60-day sweep at startup and
     at most hourly from POST /error-logs; read bounded to 60 days; PG gate EL (port 56459). Release: backend deploy (no
     migration), then OTA for the on-device redaction. Existing test rows: read-only count first, then owner picks scrub or delete.
  8. **Import vs merged-away customer:** never re-create one; skip the row with a warning pointing to the keeper (A39 follow-up). **IMPLEMENTED on branch import-followups (not pushed/deployed).**
  9. **Ingredient import:** a row with a new SKU whose exact name matches exactly one ingredient with a blank SKU ASSIGNS the SKU to
     it (no duplicate) (A39 follow-up). **IMPLEMENTED on branch import-followups (not pushed/deployed).**
  10. **OTA rollout (A35):** pilot outlet first, once more than one outlet's tablets are on APK 2.1 (staged-rollout mechanism
      to be built when that is true; until then all-at-once on the single pilot set).
  11. **Migration numbering:** A37 takes no migration. Whichever plan merges first takes 0057; any later plan renumbers in the
      same PR and updates its plan text and PG gate.
- 2026-11-08 (QA triage, `docs/plans/2026-11-08-qa-triage.md` section 4): split-order refunds keep D-56 (one payout method,
  default cash if the sale had cash) plus a refund-dialog warning when the payout exceeds what that method took; negative
  opening cash is rejected ("doesn't make sense logically"); cash entry is whole rupiah, typed and pasted alike (digits
  only, never a fraction); the split entry link reads "Split-Payment" and its picker uses the single-mode segmented style;
  held-order amount labelled "Subtotal".
- 2026-11-09 (Codex R11):
  - **F22 accepted:** when the owner tab navigates to another site, the waiting browser tab stays blocked until that
    tab is closed. There will be no fix: the browser POS is dev/QA only, the APK is unaffected, and a fix would touch
    the single-writer boundary.
  - **Open-shift report totals:** they stay a close-time snapshot, labelled "Belum dihitung" while the shift is open.
    There is no live aggregate.

## Known gaps (not P0)

- A34 release gates (2026-10-12): APK workflow has `release_type` test|production — production FAILS on debug settings
  (proven: run 38234410046 failed at the gate with exactly webContentsDebuggingEnabled + SyncProvider debug); every APK carries
  `release-manifest.json` (commit, version, sha256). OTA + backend paths gated by `scripts/lib/require-green-ci.sh` (clean
  tracked tree, HEAD on origin, 4 push-event gates green); builds/deploys come from a clean export of the tested SHA; Cloud
  Run revisions carry commit-sha + deploy-tag labels and `promote-backend.sh` re-verifies them. Open A34 leftovers: branch
  protection needs GitHub Pro (owner: spending); backoffice deploys on push (owner chose to keep); OTA release type defaults to
  test and is not stored; dependency-security's 24 blockers = A38; gate misses a backtick computed key; Git Bash rewrites
  `--env KEY=/path` values. Backoffice polls all order pages every 12 s. Backoffice polls all order pages every 12 s.
- Cloud Run runs as the default compute SA with `roles/editor` (over-privileged).
- No APK/OTA release ledger (CI artifacts expire after 90 days; OTA bucket empty) → keep APKs in `Downloads/mandalika-apk/`.
- Administrator account had 149 unexpired refresh tokens (shared login) — cleanup/review later.
- Untracked review ZIPs and `PHASE_HANDOFF.md` in the repo root/docs are leftovers (never commit them); stale worktrees
  under `..\mandalika-pos-*` and `.claude\worktrees\` can be removed.

## Working agreements (summary — full text in AGENTS.md)

Six rules (ask / scope / flag / verify / show / admit). Routing: Fable guardian only on request; Opus plan + review of
risky surfaces (every diff touching sync/outbox/stock/auth/payments/generated types, even after fixes — this session's
reviews caught several data-loss bugs); Sonnet implements, tests first; Haiku search/chores. End every reply with the
progress board. Update this file before context compaction and at each milestone.
